TL;DR: 提出一个基于大语言模型引导树搜索的自主系统，可自动生成、评估和优化传染病预测软件，在2025-2026美国呼吸道季节前瞻性评估中，其集成模型一致匹配或超越CDC人工策划的黄金标准集成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传染病预测依赖专家团队手动构建模型，效率低下，难以扩展到精细地理尺度或新兴病原体。

Method: 利用大语言模型引导的树搜索迭代生成、评估和优化可执行的预测代码，集成多种机器生成的模型，并采用对数尺度距离度量和自动评判机制避免奖励欺骗、保证结构忠实性。

Result: 系统在RSV数据稀缺的'冷启动'场景成功运行，其集成模型在样本外预测中一致匹配或超越CDC人工集成模型。

Conclusion: 该系统自主翻译流行病学理论为准确透明的代码，突破建模劳动力瓶颈，实现专家级疾病预测的快速大规模部署。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting of infectious diseases is crucial for public health but relies on labor-intensive manual model curation by expert modeling teams. This bespoke development bottlenecks scalability to granular geographic resolutions or emerging pathogens. Here, we present an autonomous system using Large Language Model (LLM)-guided tree search to iteratively generate, evaluate, and optimize executable forecasting software. In a fully prospective, real-time evaluation during the 2025-2026 US respiratory season, the system autonomously discovered methodologically diverse models for influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Aggregating these machine-generated models yielded an ensemble that consistently matched or outperformed the gold-standard, human-curated Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) hub ensembles out-of-sample. The system successfully navigated data-scarce "cold start" scenarios for RSV. Moreover, controlled retrospective ablations revealed that optimizing log-scale distance metrics prevents reward hacking, while an automated judge-in-the-loop ensures structural fidelity to complex scientific theories. By autonomously translating epidemiological theory into accurate, transparent code, this framework overcomes the modeling labor bottleneck, enabling rapid deployment of expert-level disease forecasting at unprecedented scales.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [402] [Nonlocal Optical Response and Surface Susceptibilities: A Systematic Derivation via Spatial Moment Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15716)
*Fr\'ed\'eric Zolla*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出系统理论将非局部响应核与任意弯曲界面的表面磁化率关联，并推广了Feibelman d-参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要针对平面界面，缺乏对任意弯曲界面非局部响应的系统框架。

Method: 从最一般张量非局部本构关系出发，结合空间矩展开与分布薄层极限，推导出表面磁化率χ^s（标量），并给出曲率修正项。

Result: 建立了广义麦克斯韦边界条件，通过平面、球面、圆柱面、椭球面等实例验证了形式主义。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了弯曲界面非局部响应的统一描述，曲率修正由几何不变量H和K显式表达。

Abstract: We present a systematic theory connecting the nonlocal response kernel of a homogeneous medium to its effective surface susceptibilities for an arbitrary curved interface. Starting from the most general tensorial nonlocal constitutive relation and combining a spatial moment expansion with a distributional thin-layer limit, we show that the full complexity of the interfacial response condenses, at leading order, into a single scalar: the surface susceptibility $\chi^s$, equal for the tangential and normal components of the electric field. These quantities provide a constructive generalization of the Feibelman $d$-parameters to interfaces of arbitrary curvature, and the curvature corrections, proportional to the geometric invariants $H$ (mean curvature) and $K$ (Gaussian curvature), are derived explicitly. The formalism is illustrated on a comprehensive set of analytically tractable cases (planar, spherical, cylindrical, and ellipsoidal interfaces) for several kernel choices (Gaussian, Yukawa, tensorial Lorentz). Generalized Maxwell boundary conditions are established and compared with the classical Fresnel results.

</details>


<div id='econ.GN'></div>

# econ.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [403] [Estimating Social Norm Complementarities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15405)
*Eliana La Ferrara,Cheaheon Lim,Davide Viviano*

Main category: econ.GN

TL;DR: 提出了一个社会规范选择模型，考虑技术互补性和社会互补性，利用塞拉利昂和尼日利亚的数据估计，发现女性割礼与童婚存在互补性，一夫多妻与童婚存在社会替代性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社会规范之间的相互作用以及政策干预的效果。

Method: 构建包含技术互补性和社会遵从回报的模型，使用重复截面数据估计，并模拟政策反事实。

Result: 社会回报显著；女性割礼与童婚在塞拉利昂互补性强，一夫多妻与童婚在尼日利亚呈社会替代性。

Conclusion: 模型可用于评估法律改革和社会干预的潜在效果，差异可由人类学解释。

Abstract: We develop a model of choice over social norms that allows for complementarities along two dimensions: \textit{technological}, analogous to complementarities between consumption goods, and social, capturing returns from conformity. Together, these determine whether two norms are complements, substitutes, or independent, as defined by how the equilibrium prevalence of one norm responds to a marginal shift in the utility of another. We estimate the model using repeated cross-sections from Sierra Leone and Nigeria, focusing on female genital cutting, polygyny, and child marriage. Social returns are significant across all specifications. For female genital cutting and child marriage, we find evidence of complementarities, especially strong in Sierra Leone. For polygyny and child marriage, we find evidence of social substitutability, particularly in Nigeria. We interpret these differences using insights from anthropology. Finally, we iterate the model forward to study policy counterfactuals, assessing the potential effects of legal reforms and social interventions.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [404] [Sampling pseudospectrum for data-driven matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15234)
*Caroline Wormell*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出采样伪谱方法，用于统计检验有限数据中提取的特征值是信号还是噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法从数据中提取离散特征值，但缺乏客观标准区分真实模式与有限数据伪影。

Method: 定义采样伪谱P(λ)及其估计量ĥP(λ)，通过对有限数据样本进行再处理实现统计检验。

Result: 该方法计算高效，能提供特征值位置的概率信息。

Conclusion: 为评估从有限数据中提取的模式是否为信号提供了严谨且通用的统计框架。

Abstract: Many complex systems can be reduced to their key components through spectrally decomposing matrices that capture their dynamics. These matrices can in turn be constructed from data, often by least-squares fitting: examples of algorithms to do this include Dynamical Mode Decomposition and variants, subspace identification and eigenvalue realisation algorithms. Typical outputs of these algorithms include a range of isolated, peripheral eigenvalues capturing persistent emergent patterns in the system. However, there is no objective way to assess which of these discrete eigenvalues are artefacts of finite data error, and which are reflections of a fully sampled operator. n this paper, we present a sampling pseudospectrum $P(\lambda)$, that provides probabilistic information on the behaviour of finite-data eigenvalues in the complex plane, and an estimator $\hat P(\lambda)$, which can be obtained by reprocessing our finite data sample. The estimator, which is computationally efficient to implement, allows us to test statistically for the location of the true eigenvalues. This gives us a rigorous and very general way to assess whether the patterns we extract from finite data are likely to be signal or noise.

</details>


### [405] [Amortized Energy-Based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15407)
*Hojjat Kaveh,Ricardo Baptista,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于传输的摊销贝叶斯推理方法，通过学习观测依赖的映射近似后验分布，实现快速后验采样，避免似然函数和雅可比计算，适用于非线性逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯方法（如MCMC）需为每个观测重新推理，计算成本高，而仅从联合样本推断的需求推动无似然、高效摊销方法的发展。

Method: 最小化平均能量距离目标训练传输映射，将参考测度推向前以近似后验。映射参数化为恒等加Cameron-Martin空间扰动（高斯先验），利用神经算子表示无限维情形。

Result: 在有限维和PDE约束逆问题（多孔介质流、地震反演）中，学习到的传输捕获后验多模态和主导模式，实现快速后验采样。

Conclusion: 该方法无需似然函数和密度评估，在保持理论保证的同时实现高效摊销推理，适用于重复推理场景。

Abstract: We consider amortized Bayesian inference for nonlinear inverse problems in settings where only samples from the joint distribution of parameters and observations are available. Classical methods such as Markov chain Monte Carlo require solving a new inference problem for each observation, which can be computationally prohibitive when inference must be repeated many times. We propose a transport-based approach that learns an observation-dependent map pushing forward a reference measure to approximate the posterior distribution. The map is trained by minimizing an averaged energy-distance objective between the true posterior and the learned pushforward. This formulation is likelihood-free, requiring only joint samples, and avoids density evaluation, invertibility constraints, and Jacobian determinant computations. For function-space inverse problems with Gaussian priors, we parameterize the transport map as the identity plus a perturbation in the Cameron-Martin space of the prior, preserving absolute continuity with respect to the prior. In infinite-dimensional settings, the map is represented using neural operators. We illustrate the method on a finite-dimensional nonlinear inverse problem and two PDE-constrained inverse problems arising in porous medium flow and seismic inversion. The results show that the learned transport captures posterior structure, including multimodality and dominant modes, while enabling fast posterior sampling for new observations.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [406] [Reweighting free energy profiles between universal machine learning interatomic potentials for fast consensus building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15630)
*Sauradeep Majumdar,Miguel Steiner,Johannes C. B. Dietschreit,Swagata Roy,Daniel Willimetz,Luka\v{s} Grajciar,Rafael G\'omez-Bombarelli*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的重加权框架，利用平均能隙近似克服直接指数重加权的统计崩溃，从单一机器学习势能面高效恢复多个DFT精度的自由能形貌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头算精度自由能形貌（PMF）计算昂贵；通用机器学习势能（MLIP）虽快但准确度依赖训练数据，不同MLIP间缺乏一致性。

Method: 基于单一源MLIP初始采样，采用平均能隙近似进行重加权，绕过传统直接指数重加权因体系大导致的相空间重叠不足问题，跨多个目标MLIP（PBE+D3, PBE-sol, r²SCAN, r²SCAN-D4）恢复PMF。

Result: 在601原子纳米限域电解质中，该方法产生稳定的PMF，与目标MLIP直接模拟结果一致；不同DFT参考级下均恢复高保真热力学。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种经济有效的诊断协议，能在不同MLIP间达成跨模型共识，避免冗余计算，准确获得反应和活化自由能等热力学性质。

Abstract: Free energy profiles serve as a fundamental bridge between microscopic atomic fluctuations and macroscopic thermodynamic observables. Estimating the free energy profile along a reaction coordinate, referred to as the potential of mean force (PMF), with density functional theory (DFT) accuracy is computationally expensive. Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) drastically reduce this cost, but their accuracy is strongly determined by their training data and hence can be uncertain for a given system. In this work, we present a systematic and scalable framework for reweighting PMFs, initially sampled with a single 'source' MLIP, across a representative suite of target MLIPs. Because traditional direct exponential reweighting fails for large system sizes due to low phase-space overlap between potentials, we deploy robust analytical corrections. Applying this to a complex 601-atom system of Li$^+$ transport in a nanoconfined electrolyte, we demonstrate that a mean energy-gap approximation effectively bypasses statistical collapse, producing a highly stable PMF matching the target PMF. Using this approach, we recover high-fidelity target thermodynamics across multiple DFT reference levels (PBE+D3, PBE-sol, r$^2$SCAN,r$^2$SCAN-D4) at a fraction of the computational cost of full simulations. Furthermore, thermodynamic analysis reveals that the studied MLIPs partition into two distinct clusters driven by their training data. Our reweighting framework successfully recovers target thermodynamic properties--specifically, reaction and activation free energies--even when the phase-space overlap between potentials is critically low. Ultimately, this approach establishes a vital diagnostic protocol to achieve affordable cross-model consensus on materials chemistry properties without redundant, resource-intensive simulations.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [407] [Thermodynamic Invariants of Coupled Channels: A Many-Channel Tolman-Ehrenfest Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15653)
*Benjamin Hamblin,Victor Calo,Klaus Regenauer-Lieb*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 从熵流形几何推导出多通道热力学平衡条件，解决颗粒材料60年谜题，提出可实验检验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多热力学通道耦合时单通道平衡条件失效，且颗粒材料中状态依赖的Kμ问题长期未解。

Method: 将Tolman-Ehrenfest效应扩展至熵流形，利用Ruppeiner联络的holonomy推导n通道不变量，应用于体积-应力系。

Result: 得到不变量ζ_i T_i = C，并发现Rowe剪胀比和能量限制源自几何曲率，状态依赖的Kμ问题被修正因子ζ_V解决，其在接近堵塞时达O(1)。

Conclusion: 几何方法统一了多通道平衡条件和颗粒材料行为，预测ζ_Vχ=const可通过剪切带实验检验。

Abstract: When multiple thermodynamic channels are coupled, single-channel equilibrium conditions fail. Extending the Tolman--Ehrenfest effect to the entropy manifold, we derive the unique $n$-channel invariant $\zeta_i T_i = C$, where $\zeta_i$ is the holonomy of the Ruppeiner connection. For the granular volume--stress ensemble, Rowe's dilatancy ratio and energy restriction emerge as geometric consequences of the off-diagonal curvature $g_{V\sigma}$, and the 60-year puzzle of state-dependent $K_\mu$ is resolved: the correction $\zeta_V$ reaches $O(1)$ near jamming. The prediction $\zeta_V\chi=\mathrm{const}$ across a shear band is experimentally testable.

</details>


### [408] [Spectral separation of variables from equivalent Lagrangian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15679)
*Mattia Scomparin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二次拉格朗日量动力学等价条件，通过谱分解实现运动方程解耦，应用于可积系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二次拉格朗日量等价与变量分离的关系，以简化运动方程。

Method: 推导两个二次拉格朗日量生成相同欧拉-拉格朗日方程的兼容性条件，利用谱分解解耦。

Result: 兼容性条件为动能矩阵与势能Hessian对易，导致配置空间正交谱分解，运动方程块分离或完全解耦。

Conclusion: 该方法可恢复Sawada-Kotera和Henon-Heiles等可积系统的经典可积参数区域。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical equivalence of quadratic Lagrangians and its relation to separation of variables. We show that requiring two quadratic Lagrangians to generate the same Euler--Lagrange equations imposes a compatibility condition between the kinetic matrices and the potential. For constant symmetric kinetic matrices, this condition reduces to a commutation relation with the Hessian of the potential, yielding an orthogonal spectral decomposition of the configuration space. The equations of motion then decouple into independent subsystems: generically in block-separated form, and completely when the spectrum is simple. Applications include the Sawada--Kotera system and an $n$-dimensional extension of the H\'{e}non--Heiles model, where the classical integrable parameter regimes are recovered.

</details>


### [409] [Epstein vector zeta functions related to the ADE Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16042)
*M.Olshanetsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 针对ADE型李代数的根格，引入向量值Epstein zeta函数，通过构造向量值theta级数和Mellin变换得到，推导出矩阵函数方程，并分类了满足该方程的格与不变子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Epstein zeta函数到向量值情形，并研究其函数方程的性质。

Method: 利用判别群D=P/Q构造向量值theta级数实现Weil表示，通过Mellin变换得到向量值Epstein zeta函数，并利用等变性推导矩阵函数方程。

Result: 得到Riemann型矩阵函数方程，并证明其仅对Mp(2,Z)中心元素C的不变向量子空间成立。

Conclusion: 完整分类了满足该矩阵函数方程的ADE型根格及相应的不变子空间。

Abstract: We introduce a vector-valued generalization of the Epstein zeta functions associated with the root lattices of ADE-type Lie algebras. The quadratic forms defining these lattices correspond to the Gram matrices of the simple roots. Using the discriminant group D = P/Q, we construct vector-valued theta series that realize the Weil representation of the metaplectic group Mp(2,Z). The proposed Epstein vector zeta functions are obtained as the Mellin transform of these theta series. By exploiting the equivariance properties of the theta vectors, we derive a matrix functional equation of the Riemann type. We show that the existence of this functional equation is governed by a selection rule: it holds specifically for the subspace of C-invariant vectors, where C is the central element of Mp(2,Z). Finally, we provide a complete classification of the lattices and invariant subspaces for which this matrix functional equation is satisfied.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [410] [Non-combinatorial involutive braidings: the quantum algebra $\mathfrak{gl}_{k,m}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16121)
*Anastasia Doikou*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 研究辫子方程的对合非组合解作为置换映射的变形，得到gl_{k,m} Yangian量子代数，构造最高权模并与自旋链哈密顿量关联，特例gl_{1,1}对应Heisenberg XX模型变体，组合基由杨表刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索辫子方程的非组合解，识别其背后的量子代数结构。

Method: 利用对合非组合解定义gl_{k,m} Yangian，构造最高权模并分析组合基。

Result: gl_{k,m} Yangian作为子代数，自旋链哈密顿量在gl_{1,1}时简化为XX模型变体，最高权表示基与杨表形状对应。

Conclusion: 建立了辫子方程解与Yangian代数、自旋链模型的联系，并给出了组合描述。

Abstract: We investigate involutive, non-combinatorial solutions of the braid equation viewed as special deformations of the permutation map. By employing these solutions, we identify the associated quantum algebra, which we introduce as the $\mathfrak{gl}_{k,m}$ Yangian. The algebra $\mathfrak{gl}_{k,m}$ is also recognized as a subalgebra of the Yangian. Furthermore, we construct specific highest-weight modules of $\mathfrak{gl}_{k,m},$ which simultaneously yield the eigenstates of certain quantum spin-chain-like Hamiltonians. In the special case of the algebra $\mathfrak{gl}_{1,1}$ the spin chain Hamiltonian reduces to a variant of the Heisenberg XX model. Furthermore, we present a comprehensive analysis of combinatorial bases of highest weight representations of $\mathfrak{gl}_{1,1}$, explicitly linking them to specific shapes of Young tableaux.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [411] [Clock-state olfactory search in turbulent flows using Q-learning: The geometry of plume recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15938)
*Marco Rando,Robin A. Heinonen,Yujia Qi,Agnese Seminara*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 使用表格Q-learning训练嗅觉搜索代理，仅利用上次嗅探以来的时间记忆，学习可解释策略（激增、盘旋、顺风返回），在湍流数据中表现良好，但缺乏适应性，增加灵活性可提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在湍流环境中，有效利用嗅觉观察历史进行导航以找到气味源。

Method: 采用表格Q-learning，代理记忆仅包括上次嗅探以来的时间，学习结合激增、盘旋和顺风返回的策略。

Result: 在直接数值模拟湍流数据上表现良好，但无法适应局部间歇性；提供更多灵活性可改善鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 增加策略灵活性可提升导航鲁棒性。

Abstract: Finding an odor source in a turbulent flow requires effectively leveraging the history of olfactory observations into a robust navigation strategy. In this work, we use tabular Q-learning to train an olfactory search agent with a minimal memory of past observations: only a running clock since the last whiff. This agent learns an interpretable strategy to recover the plume which combines well-known behaviors observed in insects: surging, casting, and a return downwind. While achieving good performance on data from direct numerical simulations of turbulence, the agent is limited by an inability to adapt its strategy to the local intermittency level; we show that providing more flexibility improves robustness.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [412] [Evaluating Design Video Generation: Metrics for Compositional Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16223)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Dingning Cao,Jaejung Seol,Elad Hirsch,Purvanshi Mehta*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一个面向设计动画的自动评估框架，涵盖布局保真度、运动正确性、时间质量和内容保真度四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频模型在设计动画中缺乏标准化评估框架，且设计动画有结构化约束（如指定组件运动、非动画区域稳定等），主观评估不可靠。

Method: 构建全自动评估框架，从布局保真度、运动正确性、时间质量和内容保真度四个维度量化评估。

Result: 消除了对主观人类评估的依赖，为领域内基准测试提供了共同基础。

Conclusion: 该框架可标准化评估设计动画生成模型，推动领域进步。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly used in design animation tasks, yet no standardized evaluation framework exists for this domain. Unlike natural video generation, design animation imposes structured constraints: specific components shall animate with prescribed motion types, directions, speed and timing, while non-animated regions must remain stable and layout structure must be preserved. This paper provides a fully automated evaluation framework organized across four dimensions: layout fidelity, motion correctness, temporal quality, and content fidelity. This eliminates the reliance on subjective human evaluation and establishes a common basis for benchmarking progress in the field.

</details>


### [413] [Unified Simulation of Lagrangian Particle Dynamics via Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15305)
*Caoliwen Wang,Minghao Guo,Siyuan Chen,Heng Zhang,Mengdi Wang,Xingyu Ni,Hanson Sun,Kunyi Wang,Zherong Pan,Kui Wu,Lingjie Liu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang,Taku Komura,Wojciech Matusik,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于Transformer的学习型粒子模拟器，统一模拟布料、弹性固体、牛顿与非牛顿流体、颗粒材料和分子动力学，无需为每种现象重新设计求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期目标是创建一个统一的模拟器，能够模拟多种物理现象而无需针对特定求解器重新设计。

Method: 采用预测-校正设计，在共享的拉格朗日粒子表征上，先用显式预测器在外力下推进粒子，再用学习的校正器通过三个阶段（粒子分词器、超级标记编码器、超级标记解码器）预测残差位置和速度更新，编码器通过逐渐合并标记降低注意力成本。

Result: 同一架构在六类动力学上泛化到未见过的材料、边界条件、初始条件和外力，并支持交互控制、逆向设计和从真实操作数据学习。

Conclusion: 减少了为每种现象进行求解器工程的需求，提供了一种通用模拟方法。

Abstract: A unified simulator that can model diverse physical phenomena without solver-specific redesign is a long-standing goal across simulation science. We present a learning-based particle simulator built on a single transformer architecture to model cloth, elastic solds, Newtonian and non-Newtonian fluids, granular materials, and molecular dynamics. Our model follows a prediction-correction design on a shared Lagrangian particle representation. An explicit predictor first advances particles under the known external forces, producing an intermediate state that captures externally driven motion but not inter-particle interactions. A learned corrector then predicts the residual position and velocity updates through three stages: a particle tokenizer that encodes local particle-particle, particle-boundary, and topology-guided interactions; a super-token encoder that hierarchically merges particle tokens into a compact set of super tokens via alternating self-attention and token merging; and a super-token decoder that lifts these super tokens back to particle resolution through cross-attention to predict per-particle position and velocity corrections. Progressive token merging reduces the attention cost at successive encoder layers by halving the token count at each level, and the decoder communicates through the compact super-token set rather than full particle-to-particle attention. Across the six dynamics categories, the same architecture generalizes to unseen materials, boundary configurations, initial conditions, and external forces. We further demonstrate downstream interactive control, inverse design, and learning from real-world manipulation data, reducing the need for per-phenomenon solver engineering.

</details>


### [414] [FFAvatar: Few-Shot, Feed-Forward, and Generalizable Avatar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15320)
*Thuan Hoang Nguyen,Jiahao Luo,Yinyu Nie,Hao Li,Gordon Guocheng Qian,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: FFAvatar是一个前馈框架，能从少量无姿态肖像图像中快速重建高保真、可动画的3D高斯头像，通过多视图查询变换器和三步训练课程实现，重建仅需2-10秒，动画速度达49 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统头像重建依赖每主体优化或昂贵预处理，耗时且可扩展性差，需要更快速通用的方法。

Method: 使用多视图查询变换器将多源图像融合为统一规范高斯表示，通过端到端预测FLAME参数驱动动画，并采用三步训练：大规模单眼视频预训练、多视角微调、可选个性化优化。

Result: 在NeRSemble基准上PSNR比LAM提升5.5，重建时间2秒（无个性化）或10秒（有个性化），支持49 FPS实时动画。

Conclusion: FFAvatar在身份保持、几何一致性和动画保真度上达到新标准，同时实现快速重建与实时部署。

Abstract: Avatar reconstruction has traditionally relied on per-subject optimization that requires hours of computation or on expensive preprocessing that limits scalability. We introduce FFAvatar, a generalizable feed-forward framework that reconstructs high-quality, animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars from few-shot unposed portrait images in seconds. FFAvatar fuses information from multiple source images into a unified canonical Gaussian representation through Multi-View Query-Former, which is animated via FLAME parameters predicted end-to-end directly from pixels, eliminating the overhead of offline FLAME extraction. We further propose a three-stage training curriculum that achieves both broad generalization and high-fidelity reconstruction: (i) scalable pretraining on extensive monocular video data with over 1M identities to learn strong generalizable priors; (ii) multi-view fine-tuning on a small but high-quality dataset of 360-degree captures to enhance geometric fidelity and extreme-view awareness; and (iii) optional personalization that adapts to specific identities for maximum fidelity within 500 optimization steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FFAvatar sets a new standard for identity preservation, geometric consistency, and animation fidelity. On the NeRSemble benchmark, it outperforms the state-of-the-art LAM by a substantial 5.5 PSNR gain. Furthermore, FFAvatar enables real-time deployment, reconstructing avatars in 2 seconds without personalization and 10 seconds with personalization, while supporting 49 FPS animation on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU.

</details>


### [415] [StippleDiffusion: Capacity-Constrained Stippling using Controlled Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15816)
*Ofir Gilad,Aleksander Plocharski,Przemyslaw Musialski,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Stipple patterns, point sets whose local density tracks a target image, are traditionally produced by per-density iterative optimizers, which are slow, non-differentiable, and must be re-run from scratch for each new target. Learned alternatives have so far addressed only unconditional point generation; capacity-constrained, image-conditioned stippling has remained out of reach. We present the first diffusion-based sampler that simultaneously satisfies a learned local point-distribution prior and a continuous, image-defined capacity constraint at inference. The method is a ControlNet branch built on top of an optimal-transport-grid point-set diffusion baseline, conditioned on the target density map and a high-resolution image. Two design choices make the combination tractable: training and inference are restricted to the late-stage denoising regime, initialized from a density-weighted rejection sample, and the standard zero-convolution injection is replaced with a sigmoid-gated 1x1 projection that preserves the base model's blue-noise structure under hard density signals. A single trained checkpoint accepts arbitrary target densities at inference, generalizes to point budgets that were not seen during training, and produces stipples in time nearly independent of the output point count. On the Icons-50 benchmark, our learned sampler reaches parity with per-density-optimized baselines on every reported metric while remaining differentiable end-to-end.

</details>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [416] [Tweedie's Formula, Variance Functions, and Score-Driven Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15902)
*Peter Reinhard Hansen,Chen Tong*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯角度解释了分数驱动模型，通过Tweedie公式将条件似然分数与时变参数更新联系起来，并展示了在共轭自然指数族中与经典折扣贝叶斯递归的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分数驱动模型提供统一的贝叶斯解释，并揭示其与经验贝叶斯、近似滤波和动态广义线性模型的关系。

Method: 利用Tweedie公式和Fisher评分，推导出逆Fisher缩放的条件分数作为局部高斯后验修正，并在共轭自然指数族中建立精确的分数驱动表示。

Result: 在Gaussian信号提取中，后验修正等于边际密度分数的缩放；在一般分布中，局部高斯后验修正通过Fisher评分和精度折扣实现。

Conclusion: 分数驱动模型可视为精确贝叶斯更新的局部近似，连接了多种统计模型，并区分了精确更新与近似方法。

Abstract: Score-driven models update time-varying parameters using conditional likelihood scores. This paper gives a Bayesian interpretation based on Tweedie's formula. In Gaussian signal extraction, Tweedie's formula expresses the posterior correction as a scaled score of the marginal predictive density; in natural exponential families, the corresponding identity includes a base-measure adjustment. For general conditional densities, we show that inverse-Fisher-scaled conditional scores arise as local Gaussian posterior corrections based on Fisher scoring and precision discounting. For conjugate natural exponential families, the classical discounted Bayesian recursion has an exact score-driven representation: with steady-state precision discounting and expectation-space inverse-Fisher scaling, the score-driven correction equals the Bayesian posterior mean before transition dynamics are imposed. Tweedie's variance-function index further clarifies how conditional scores normalize forecast errors. The results link empirical Bayes, approximate filtering, dynamic generalized linear models, and score-driven models while distinguishing exact Bayesian updating from local score-based approximation.

</details>


### [417] [Quasi-Bayesian Local Projection Instrumental-Variables Method: Application to Renewable Energy and Electricity Prices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15966)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于局部投影工具变量估计的准贝叶斯方法，利用GMM目标构建矩条件准后验，并采用粗糙度惩罚先验平滑不同期限的脉冲响应，在保持传统方法一阶特性的同时提高有限样本稳定性，支持通过同时置信带进行联合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部投影工具变量方法在有限样本中稳定性不足，且难以进行联合推断。

Method: 基于GMM目标构建准后验，施加粗糙度惩罚先验以平滑脉冲响应，采用准贝叶斯估计。

Result: 模拟表明，与标准GMM相比，该方法降低了均方根误差，尤其在中期和长期。

Conclusion: 应用于丹麦电力市场的实例验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a quasi-Bayesian approach for local projection instrumental-variables (LP-IV) estimation. It builds a moment-based quasi-posterior using the generalized method of moments (GMM) objective and applies a roughness-penalty prior to smooth impulse responses over different horizons. The approach maintains the key first-order features of traditional LP-IV methods, while enhancing stability in finite samples and allowing for joint inference through simultaneous bands. Simulations indicate that this regularization decreases root mean squared error compared to standard GMM, especially at medium and longer horizons. An application to Danish electricity markets highlights the method's practical usefulness.

</details>
